`test/adapters/ton/builder.test.ts`:

```ts
import { ed25519 } from '@noble/curves/ed25519';
import {
  createTonBroadcaster,
  createTonBuilder,
  REQUEST_ID,
} from '../../../src/adapters/ton/builder';
import type { BuildContext } from '../../../src/core/driver/types';
import type { DriverIntent } from '../../../src/core/model/intent';
import { tonHarness } from './support/context';
import { testWallet } from './support/harness';
import { NODE_FEES } from './support/node';
import { KEY, PUBLIC_KEY } from './support/vectors';

const TESTNET = -3;
const GRAM = 1_000_000_000n;
const FRESH = `0:${'11'.repeat(32)}`;
const MASTER = `0:${'77'.repeat(32)}`;
const PK = Buffer.from(PUBLIC_KEY, 'hex');

function setup(version: 'v4r2' | 'v5r1' = 'v4r2') {
  const h = tonHarness();
  const from = testWallet(version, TESTNET);
  const builder = createTonBuilder(h.ctx);
  const broadcaster = createTonBroadcaster(h.ctx);
  const build = (
    seqno = 0n,
    wallet: Readonly<Record<string, unknown>> = { ton: { version } },
  ): BuildContext => ({
    from,
    keys: [{ scheme: 'ed25519', publicKey: PK }],
    wallet,
    ordering: { kind: 'seqno', seqno, validUntil: 0 },
  });
  const intent = (patch: Partial<DriverIntent> = {}): DriverIntent => ({
    asset: 'native',
    outputs: [
      {
        to: FRESH,
        amount: GRAM,
        variant: { bounceable: false, testOnly: true, urlSafe: true },
      },
    ],
    from,
    fee: 'normal',
    ...patch,
  });
  /** estimate → check → build → sign → assemble, as the engine does. */
  const prepare = async (i: DriverIntent, b = build()) => {
    const fee = await h.run(builder.estimateFee(i, b));
    const funds = await h.run(builder.checkFunds(i, fee, b));
    const unsigned = await h.run(builder.build(i, fee, b));
    const request = unsigned.signingRequests[0]!;
    const signature = ed25519.sign(request.payload, Buffer.from(KEY, 'hex'));
    const signed = await builder.assemble(unsigned, [
      { requestId: request.id, bytes: signature },
    ]);
    return { fee, funds, unsigned, signed };
  };
  return { h, from, builder, broadcaster, build, intent, prepare };
}

describe('the TON builder', () => {
  it.each(['v4r2', 'v5r1'] as const)(
    'deploys a %s wallet with its first transfer and pays a non-bounceable recipient',
    async (version) => {
      const s = setup(version);
      s.h.node.fund(s.from, 3n * GRAM);
      const { fee, funds, unsigned, signed } = await s.prepare(
        s.intent({ memo: 'invoice 7' }),
      );
      const gas =
        (version === 'v4r2' ? NODE_FEES.gasV4 : NODE_FEES.gasV5) + NODE_FEES.deployGas;
      expect(fee).toMatchObject({
        kind: 'ton',
        bound: 'expected',
        payer: s.from,
        details: { importFee: NODE_FEES.importFee, gasFee: gas, deploy: true },
      });
      expect(fee.charges[0]?.amount).toBeGreaterThan(NODE_FEES.importFee + gas);
      expect(funds).toEqual({ ok: true });
      const now = Math.floor(s.h.clock.now() / 1000);
      expect(unsigned).toMatchObject({
        payload: { encoding: 'base64' },
        ordering: { kind: 'seqno', seqno: 0n, validUntil: now + 60 },
        signingRequests: [
          { id: REQUEST_ID, scheme: 'ed25519', payloadKind: 'message', publicKey: PK },
        ],
        summary: {
          asset: 'ton:testnet/native',
          outputs: [{ to: FRESH, amount: String(GRAM) }],
          memo: 'invoice 7',
        },
      });
      expect(signed.ref).toMatchObject({ idKind: 'message-hash', canonical: false });
      expect(await s.h.run(s.broadcaster.broadcast(signed))).toEqual({
        kind: 'accepted',
      });
      expect(s.h.node.sendCount(signed.ref.id)).toBe(1);
      s.h.node.mine(2);
      expect(s.h.node.balance(FRESH)).toBe(GRAM);
      expect(s.h.node.seqno(s.from)).toBe(1);
    },
  );

  it('sends a raw recipient bounceable, and the next transfer without a StateInit', async () => {
    const s = setup();
    s.h.node.fund(s.from, 5n * GRAM);
    const first = await s.prepare(s.intent({ outputs: [{ to: FRESH, amount: GRAM }] }));
    await s.h.run(s.broadcaster.broadcast(first.signed));
    s.h.node.mine(3);
    expect(s.h.node.balance(FRESH)).toBe(0n);
    const second = await s.prepare(s.intent(), s.build(1n));
    expect(second.fee.details).toMatchObject({ deploy: false });
    await s.h.run(s.broadcaster.broadcast(second.signed));
    s.h.node.mine(2);
    expect(s.h.node.balance(FRESH)).toBe(GRAM);
  });

  it('refuses a wallet identity that does not derive the sender, before any I/O (lesson 5)', async () => {
    const s = setup();
    const before = s.h.node.served.length;
    await expect(
      s.h.run(
        s.builder.estimateFee(s.intent(), s.build(0n, { ton: { version: 'v5r1' } })),
      ),
    ).rejects.toMatchObject({ code: 'CONFIG_INVALID' });
    await expect(
      s.h.run(s.builder.build(s.intent(), {} as never, s.build(0n, {}))),
    ).rejects.toMatchObject({ code: 'CONFIG_INVALID' });
    expect(s.h.node.served.length).toBe(before);
  });

  it('refuses more messages than the wallet version sends, and an oversized memo', async () => {
    const s = setup();
    const five = Array.from({ length: 5 }, () => ({ to: FRESH, amount: 1n }));
    await expect(
      s.h.run(s.builder.estimateFee(s.intent({ outputs: five }), s.build())),
    ).rejects.toMatchObject({
      code: 'INVALID_INTENT',
      message: expect.stringContaining('at most 4'),
    });
    await expect(
      s.h.run(s.builder.estimateFee(s.intent({ memo: 'x'.repeat(1025) }), s.build())),
    ).rejects.toMatchObject({ code: 'INVALID_INTENT' });
    await expect(
      s.h.run(s.builder.estimateFee(s.intent({ fee: { attached: 1n } }), s.build())),
    ).rejects.toMatchObject({ code: 'INVALID_INTENT' });
  });

  it('reports a shortfall with required and available amounts', async () => {
    const s = setup();
    s.h.node.fund(s.from, GRAM);
    const i = s.intent();
    const fee = await s.h.run(s.builder.estimateFee(i, s.build()));
    const funds = await s.h.run(s.builder.checkFunds(i, fee, s.build()));
    expect(funds).toMatchObject({ ok: false, asset: 'native', available: GRAM });
    expect(funds.ok === false && funds.required > GRAM).toBe(true);
  });

  it('refuses to build on a frozen wallet', async () => {
    const s = setup();
    s.h.node.fund(s.from, GRAM);
    s.h.node.freeze(s.from);
    await expect(
      s.h.run(s.builder.estimateFee(s.intent(), s.build())),
    ).rejects.toMatchObject({
      code: 'TX_REFUSED',
    });
  });

  it('moves jettons with the attached value as an upper bound, refunding the excess', async () => {
    const s = setup();
    s.h.node.fund(s.from, 2n * GRAM);
    s.h.node.deployJetton(MASTER, { symbol: 'TST', decimals: 6, content: 'onchain' });
    s.h.node.mintJetton(MASTER, s.from, 1_000n);
    const jetton = { standard: 'jetton', contract: MASTER };
    const short = s.intent({ asset: jetton, outputs: [{ to: FRESH, amount: 5_000n }] });
    const shortFee = await s.h.run(s.builder.estimateFee(short, s.build()));
    expect(await s.h.run(s.builder.checkFunds(short, shortFee, s.build()))).toEqual({
      ok: false,
      asset: jetton,
      required: 5_000n,
      available: 1_000n,
    });
    const i = s.intent({
      asset: jetton,
      outputs: [{ to: FRESH, amount: 400n }],
      memo: 'order 9',
      fee: { attached: 60_000_000n },
    });
    const { fee, unsigned, signed } = await s.prepare(i);
    expect(fee).toMatchObject({
      bound: 'upper',
      charges: [{ label: 'network' }, { label: 'attached', amount: 60_000_000n }],
      details: { attached: 60_000_000n, forwardAmount: 1n },
    });
    expect(unsigned.summary.asset).toBe(`ton:testnet/jetton:${MASTER}`);
    await s.h.run(s.broadcaster.broadcast(signed));
    s.h.node.mine(5);
    expect(s.h.node.jettonBalance(MASTER, FRESH)).toBe(400n);
    expect(s.h.node.balance(FRESH)).toBe(1n);
  });

  it('assembles only with the right signature, into the only position it fits', async () => {
    const s = setup();
    s.h.node.fund(s.from, GRAM);
    const { unsigned } = await s.prepare(s.intent());
    await expect(s.builder.assemble(unsigned, [])).rejects.toMatchObject({
      code: 'SIGNING_FAILED',
    });
    await expect(
      s.builder.assemble(unsigned, [
        { requestId: REQUEST_ID, bytes: new Uint8Array(63) },
      ]),
    ).rejects.toMatchObject({ code: 'SIGNING_FAILED' });
    const tampered = {
      ...unsigned,
      signingRequests: [{ ...unsigned.signingRequests[0]!, payload: new Uint8Array(32) }],
    };
    await expect(
      s.builder.assemble(tampered, [
        { requestId: REQUEST_ID, bytes: new Uint8Array(64) },
      ]),
    ).rejects.toMatchObject({ code: 'SIGNING_FAILED' });
  });
});

describe('the TON broadcaster', () => {
  it('rejects bytes that are no external message without sending them', async () => {
    const s = setup();
    const before = s.h.node.served.length;
    for (const raw of [
      { encoding: 'base64' as const, data: 'bm90IGEgYm9j' },
      { encoding: 'hex' as const, data: 'ab' },
    ]) {
      expect(
        await s.h.run(
          s.broadcaster.broadcast({
            raw,
            ref: { id: '', idKind: 'message-hash', canonical: false },
          }),
        ),
      ).toEqual({
        kind: 'rejected',
        reason: 'malformed message',
      });
    }
    expect(s.h.node.served.length).toBe(before);
  });

  it("rethrows toncenter's HTTP 500 refusal unclassified and ambiguous (D16)", async () => {
    const s = setup();
    s.h.node.fund(s.from, GRAM);
    const { signed } = await s.prepare(s.intent(), s.build(5n));
    await expect(s.h.run(s.broadcaster.broadcast(signed))).rejects.toMatchObject({
      code: 'PROVIDER_UNAVAILABLE',
      ambiguous: true,
    });
  });

  it('classifies a definitive 4xx answer, passing fanout and signal through', async () => {
    const s = setup();
    s.h.node.fund(s.from, GRAM);
    const { signed } = await s.prepare(s.intent());
    const seen: unknown[] = [];
    const http = s.h.rpc.http.bind(s.h.rpc);
    s.h.rpc.http = ((request, options) => {
      seen.push({
        route: request.route,
        purpose: options?.purpose,
        retry: options?.retry,
        fanout: options?.fanout,
        signal: options?.signal !== undefined,
      });
      return http(request, options);
    }) as typeof s.h.rpc.http;
    s.h.node.intercept = (_e, route) =>
      route === '/sendBocReturnHash'
        ? {
            status: 400,
            json: {
              ok: false,
              error: 'External message was not accepted: exitcode=33',
              code: 400,
            },
          }
        : undefined;
    const signal = new AbortController().signal;
    expect(await s.h.run(s.broadcaster.broadcast(signed, { fanout: 2, signal }))).toEqual(
      {
        kind: 'refused',
        code: 'NONCE_CONFLICT',
        reason: 'seqno mismatch',
      },
    );
    expect(seen).toEqual([
      {
        route: '/sendBocReturnHash',
        purpose: 'broadcast',
        retry: 'ambiguous-on-failure',
        fanout: 2,
        signal: true,
      },
    ]);
    s.h.node.intercept = (_e, route) =>
      route === '/sendBocReturnHash'
        ? {
            status: 400,
            json: { ok: false, error: 'duplicate external message', code: 400 },
          }
        : undefined;
    expect(await s.h.run(s.broadcaster.broadcast(signed))).toEqual({
      kind: 'already-known',
    });
  });
});

describe('the TON builder: fees, chain time and jetton wallets', () => {
  it("counts each forward fee once: the emulation's, else the config's (I3)", async () => {
    const s = setup();
    s.h.node.fund(s.from, 3n * GRAM);
    const i = s.intent({ memo: 'invoice 7' });
    const fee = await s.h.run(s.builder.estimateFee(i, s.build()));
    const details = fee.details as unknown as {
      importFee: bigint;
      gasFee: bigint;
      storageFee: bigint;
      forwardFee: bigint;
      forwardFeeSource: string;
    };
    expect(details.forwardFeeSource).toBe('emulated');
    expect(details.forwardFee).toBeGreaterThan(0n);
    expect(fee.charges).toEqual([
      {
        asset: 'native',
        label: 'network',
        amount:
          details.importFee + details.gasFee + details.storageFee + details.forwardFee,
      },
    ]);
    // An endpoint that reports no forward fee: the config's formula, still counted once.
    s.h.node.intercept = (_e, route) =>
      route === '/estimateFee'
        ? {
            json: {
              ok: true,
              result: {
                source_fees: { in_fwd_fee: 1, storage_fee: 0, gas_fee: 2, fwd_fee: 0 },
                destination_fees: [],
              },
            },
          }
        : undefined;
    const computed = await s.h.run(s.builder.estimateFee(i, s.build()));
    expect(computed.details).toMatchObject({
      forwardFee: details.forwardFee,
      forwardFeeSource: 'computed',
    });
  });

  it('refuses a chain time far from the local clock (M3)', async () => {
    const s = setup();
    s.h.node.fund(s.from, GRAM);
    const fee = await s.h.run(s.builder.estimateFee(s.intent(), s.build()));
    s.h.node.intercept = (_e, route, request) => {
      if (route !== '/getAddressInformation') return undefined;
      s.h.node.intercept = undefined;
      return s.h.node.fetch.fetch(request.url.href).then(async (response) => {
        const body = (await response.json()) as { result: { sync_utime: number } };
        body.result.sync_utime += 3_600;
        return { json: body };
      }) as never;
    };
    await expect(
      s.h.run(s.builder.build(s.intent(), fee, s.build())),
    ).rejects.toMatchObject({
      code: 'PROVIDER_INCONSISTENT',
      retryable: true,
    });
  });

  it('sends jettons to the wallet the proof quorum named (I5)', async () => {
    const s = setup();
    s.h.node.fund(s.from, 2n * GRAM);
    s.h.node.deployJetton(MASTER, { symbol: 'TST', decimals: 6, content: 'onchain' });
    s.h.node.mintJetton(MASTER, s.from, 1_000n);
    const purposes: string[] = [];
    const http = s.h.rpc.http.bind(s.h.rpc);
    s.h.rpc.http = ((request, options) => {
      if (request.route === '/runGetMethod') purposes.push(String(options?.purpose));
      return http(request, options);
    }) as typeof s.h.rpc.http;
    const i = s.intent({
      asset: { standard: 'jetton', contract: MASTER },
      outputs: [{ to: FRESH, amount: 1n }],
    });
    await s.h.run(s.builder.estimateFee(i, s.build()));
    // The first `get_wallet_address` goes to the quorum; afterwards it is cached.
    expect(purposes[0]).toBe('proof');
    expect(s.h.ctx.jettonWallets.get(`${MASTER}|${s.from}`)).toBe(
      s.h.node.jettonWalletOf(MASTER, s.from),
    );
  });
});
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Write the builder and broadcaster**

`src/adapters/ton/builder.ts`:

```ts
/**
 * TON transfers (spec §7, §15): fees, funds, the unsigned external request, its assembly,
 * and the broadcaster. Builds use `read` tags; sending uses `broadcast` /
 * `ambiguous-on-failure` with the caller's fanout and signal (R41).
 */
import {
  Cell,
  beginCell,
  loadMessage,
  storeMessageRelaxed,
  type MessageRelaxed,
} from '@ton/core';
import {
  computeMessageForwardFees,
  configParseMsgPrices,
  type MsgPrices,
} from '@ton/ton';
import type {
  BroadcastResult,
  BuildContext,
  Broadcaster,
  FundsCheck,
  TxBuilder,
  WalletKey,
} from '../../core/driver/types';
import {
  ChainError,
  ConfigError,
  ProviderError,
  SigningError,
  ValidationError,
  isCryptoAioError,
} from '../../core/errors/error';
import { assetId, type AssetRef } from '../../core/model/asset';
import type { FeeEstimateDraft } from '../../core/model/fee';
import type { DriverIntent } from '../../core/model/intent';
import type { SigningRequest } from '../../core/signing/types';
import type { UnsignedTx } from '../../core/model/transaction';
import { BROADCAST, PROOF, READ, type AccountState } from './api';
import { classifyBroadcastError } from './errors';
import { feeRequest, tonFeeDetails, tonFeeDraft, type FeeRequest } from './fees';
import { MAX_MEMO_BYTES, jettonMessage, memoBytes, nativeMessage } from './messages';
import {
  jettonBalance,
  jettonMaster,
  jettonWalletAddress,
  type TonContext,
} from './reader';
import type { TonFeeDetails } from './types';
import {
  MAX_MESSAGES,
  normalizedHash,
  resolveIdentity,
  signedRequest,
  unsignedRequest,
  walletAddress,
  walletStateInit,
  type TonIdentity,
} from './wallets';

/** The id of the one signing request (spec §15: 1 × ed25519 over the signing cell hash). */
export const REQUEST_ID = 'wallet';

const hex = (bytes: Uint8Array): string => Buffer.from(bytes).toString('hex');

interface Plan {
  readonly identity: TonIdentity;
  readonly key: WalletKey;
  readonly jetton?: { readonly master: string; readonly asset: AssetRef };
  readonly request: FeeRequest;
}

/**
 * The checks every builder method runs before any codec or signing work (lesson 5): the
 * wallet identity must derive exactly `from`, and the intent must fit the wallet.
 */
function planOf(ctx: TonContext, intent: DriverIntent, build: BuildContext): Plan {
  const identity = resolveIdentity(build.wallet, ctx.config.globalId);
  const key = build.keys.find((k) => k.scheme === 'ed25519');
  if (!key) throw new ConfigError('CONFIG_INVALID', 'the TON wallet has no ed25519 key');
  if (
    walletAddress(identity, key.publicKey) !== build.from ||
    intent.from !== build.from
  ) {
    throw new ConfigError(
      'CONFIG_INVALID',
      'the TON wallet identity does not derive the sending address',
    );
  }
  const limit = MAX_MESSAGES[identity.version];
  if (intent.outputs.length > limit) {
    throw new ValidationError(
      'INVALID_INTENT',
      `a ${identity.version} wallet sends at most ${limit} messages per transfer`,
    );
  }
  if (intent.memo !== undefined && memoBytes(intent.memo) > MAX_MEMO_BYTES) {
    throw new ValidationError(
      'INVALID_INTENT',
      `a TON memo is at most ${MAX_MEMO_BYTES} bytes`,
    );
  }
  const asset = intent.asset;
  let jetton: Plan['jetton'];
  if (asset !== 'native') {
    const master = jettonMaster(ctx, asset);
    jetton = { master, asset: { standard: 'jetton', contract: master } };
  }
  return {
    identity,
    key,
    ...(jetton ? { jetton } : {}),
    request: feeRequest(intent.fee, jetton !== undefined),
  };
}

/** How far an endpoint's `sync_utime` may be from the local clock (M3), in seconds. */
export const CHAIN_TIME_TOLERANCE = 300;

async function walletState(ctx: TonContext, from: string): Promise<AccountState> {
  const state = await ctx.api.account(from, READ);
  if (state.status === 'frozen') {
    throw new ChainError('TX_REFUSED', 'the wallet account is frozen');
  }
  return state;
}

/** The recipient's bounce flag (spec §6.4, P6-1); a raw address is bounceable. */
function bounceOf(output: DriverIntent['outputs'][number]): boolean {
  const flag = output.variant?.bounceable;
  return typeof flag === 'boolean' ? flag : true;
}

async function messagesOf(
  ctx: TonContext,
  intent: DriverIntent,
  plan: Plan,
  from: string,
  queryId: bigint,
): Promise<{ readonly messages: MessageRelaxed[]; readonly attached?: bigint }> {
  if (!plan.jetton) {
    return {
      messages: intent.outputs.map((o) =>
        nativeMessage({
          to: o.to,
          value: o.amount,
          bounce: bounceOf(o),
          ...(intent.memo !== undefined ? { memo: intent.memo } : {}),
        }),
      ),
    };
  }
  const attached = plan.request.attached ?? ctx.config.jettonAttached;
  // I5: the jetton wallet we send to is attested by the proof quorum (and cached only so).
  const jettonWallet = await jettonWalletAddress(ctx, plan.jetton.master, from, PROOF);
  return {
    attached,
    messages: intent.outputs.map((o) =>
      jettonMessage({
        jettonWallet,
        attached,
        queryId,
        amount: o.amount,
        destination: o.to,
        responseDestination: from,
        forwardAmount: ctx.config.jettonForwardAmount,
        ...(intent.memo !== undefined ? { memo: intent.memo } : {}),
      }),
    ),
  };
}

/** Message prices for these messages: config param 24 when the masterchain is involved. */
async function forwardFees(
  ctx: TonContext,
  from: string,
  messages: readonly MessageRelaxed[],
): Promise<bigint> {
  const masterchain =
    from.startsWith('-1:') ||
    messages.some((m) => m.info.type === 'internal' && m.info.dest.workChain === -1);
  const boc = await ctx.api.configParam(masterchain ? 24 : 25, READ);
  const prices: MsgPrices = configParseMsgPrices(
    (Cell.fromBoc(Buffer.from(boc, 'base64'))[0] as Cell).beginParse(),
  );
  let total = 0n;
  for (const message of messages) {
    const cell = beginCell().store(storeMessageRelaxed(message)).endCell();
    const { fees, remaining } = computeMessageForwardFees(prices, cell);
    total += fees + remaining;
  }
  return total;
}

const seqnoOf = (build: BuildContext): number => {
  const ordering = build.ordering;
  if (ordering?.kind !== 'seqno' || ordering.seqno > 0xffffffffn || ordering.seqno < 0n) {
    throw new ValidationError(
      'INVALID_INTENT',
      'a TON transfer needs its allocated seqno',
    );
  }
  return Number(ordering.seqno);
};

export function createTonBuilder(ctx: TonContext): TxBuilder {
  const { api, config } = ctx;
  return {
    async estimateFee(intent, build): Promise<FeeEstimateDraft> {
      const plan = planOf(ctx, intent, build);
      const state = await walletState(ctx, build.from);
      const deploy = state.status === 'uninitialized';
      const seqno = build.ordering?.kind === 'seqno' ? Number(build.ordering.seqno) : 0;
      const { messages, attached } = await messagesOf(
        ctx,
        intent,
        plan,
        build.from,
        BigInt(seqno),
      );
      const unsigned = await unsignedRequest(plan.identity, plan.key.publicKey, {
        seqno,
        validUntil: state.syncUtime + config.validForSeconds,
        messages,
        deploy: false,
      });
      const init = deploy
        ? walletStateInit(plan.identity, plan.key.publicKey)
        : undefined;
      const emulated = await api.estimateFee(
        {
          address: build.from,
          body: loadMessage(unsigned.message.beginParse())
            .body.toBoc()
            .toString('base64'),
          ...(init?.code && init.data
            ? {
                initCode: init.code.toBoc().toString('base64'),
                initData: init.data.toBoc().toString('base64'),
              }
            : {}),
        },
        READ,
      );
      const details: TonFeeDetails = {
        importFee: emulated.importFee,
        gasFee: emulated.gasFee,
        storageFee: emulated.storageFee,
        // I3: counted once. The emulation's `fwd_fee` follows the real action list; config
        // params 24/25 serve only when an endpoint reports none.
        ...(emulated.forwardFee > 0n
          ? { forwardFee: emulated.forwardFee, forwardFeeSource: 'emulated' as const }
          : {
              forwardFee: await forwardFees(ctx, build.from, messages),
              forwardFeeSource: 'computed' as const,
            }),
        deploy,
        ...(attached !== undefined
          ? { attached, forwardAmount: config.jettonForwardAmount }
          : {}),
      };
      return tonFeeDraft({
        speed: plan.request.speed,
        details,
        ...(plan.jetton ? { jettonOutputs: intent.outputs.length } : {}),
        payer: build.from,
      });
    },

    async checkFunds(intent, fee, build): Promise<FundsCheck> {
      const plan = planOf(ctx, intent, build);
      const native = fee.charges
        .filter((c) => c.asset === 'native')
        .reduce((sum, c) => sum + c.amount, 0n);
      const available = (await walletState(ctx, build.from)).balance;
      const sent = plan.jetton
        ? 0n
        : intent.outputs.reduce((sum, o) => sum + o.amount, 0n);
      if (available < native + sent) {
        return { ok: false, asset: 'native', required: native + sent, available };
      }
      if (plan.jetton) {
        const required = intent.outputs.reduce((sum, o) => sum + o.amount, 0n);
        const held = await jettonBalance(ctx, plan.jetton.master, build.from, READ);
        if (held < required) {
          return { ok: false, asset: plan.jetton.asset, required, available: held };
        }
      }
      return { ok: true };
    },

    async build(intent, fee, build): Promise<UnsignedTx> {
      const plan = planOf(ctx, intent, build);
      const seqno = seqnoOf(build);
      const state = await walletState(ctx, build.from);
      // M3: one answer sets a lifetime the core then waits out: bound it by the local clock.
      if (Math.abs(state.syncUtime - ctx.clock.now() / 1000) > CHAIN_TIME_TOLERANCE) {
        throw new ProviderError(
          'PROVIDER_INCONSISTENT',
          'the endpoint reports a chain time far from the local clock',
          { retryable: true },
        );
      }
      const validUntil = state.syncUtime + config.validForSeconds;
      const details = tonFeeDetails(fee.details);
      const { messages } = await messagesOf(
        ctx,
        intent,
        {
          ...plan,
          request: {
            ...plan.request,
            ...(details.attached !== undefined ? { attached: details.attached } : {}),
          },
        },
        build.from,
        BigInt(seqno),
      );
      const unsigned = await unsignedRequest(plan.identity, plan.key.publicKey, {
        seqno,
        validUntil,
        messages,
        deploy: state.status === 'uninitialized',
      });
      const request: SigningRequest = {
        id: REQUEST_ID,
        scheme: 'ed25519',
        payload: unsigned.digest,
        payloadKind: 'message',
        publicKey: plan.key.publicKey,
        ...(plan.key.keyRef ? { keyRef: plan.key.keyRef } : {}),
      };
      return {
        payload: {
          encoding: 'base64',
          data: unsigned.message.toBoc().toString('base64'),
        },
        signingRequests: [request],
        ordering: { kind: 'seqno', seqno: BigInt(seqno), validUntil },
        fee,
        summary: {
          asset: assetId(ctx.chain.id, ctx.network.id, plan.jetton?.asset ?? 'native'),
          outputs: intent.outputs.map((o) => ({ to: o.to, amount: o.amount.toString() })),
          ...(intent.memo !== undefined ? { memo: intent.memo } : {}),
        },
      };
    },

    async assemble(unsigned, signatures) {
      const request = unsigned.signingRequests[0];
      const signature = signatures.find((s) => s.requestId === request?.id);
      if (!request || !signature || signature.bytes.length !== 64) {
        throw new SigningError('SIGNING_FAILED', 'the wallet signature is missing');
      }
      let payload: Cell;
      try {
        payload = Cell.fromBoc(Buffer.from(unsigned.payload.data, 'base64'))[0] as Cell;
      } catch {
        throw new SigningError(
          'SIGNING_FAILED',
          'the unsigned TON payload does not parse',
        );
      }
      const signed = signedRequest(payload, request.payload, signature.bytes);
      return {
        raw: { encoding: 'base64', data: signed.toBoc().toString('base64') },
        ref: {
          id: hex(normalizedHash(signed)),
          idKind: 'message-hash',
          canonical: false,
        },
      };
    },
  };
}

const MALFORMED: BroadcastResult = Object.freeze({
  kind: 'rejected',
  reason: 'malformed message',
});

/** The normalized hash of raw bytes, or null when they are no external message. */
function hashOfRaw(data: string): string | null {
  try {
    const cells = Cell.fromBoc(Buffer.from(data, 'base64'));
    return cells.length === 1 ? hex(normalizedHash(cells[0] as Cell)) : null;
  } catch {
    return null;
  }
}

/** The node's own error text inside a v2 error body (`{ ok: false, error }`), if any. */
function nodeText(body: unknown): string | undefined {
  if (typeof body !== 'string') return undefined;
  try {
    const parsed = JSON.parse(body) as unknown;
    if (parsed !== null && typeof parsed === 'object') {
      const text = (parsed as Record<string, unknown>).error;
      if (typeof text === 'string') return text;
    }
  } catch {
    // Not JSON: the body itself is the text.
  }
  return body;
}

export function createTonBroadcaster(ctx: TonContext): Broadcaster {
  return {
    async broadcast(signed, options = {}) {
      // Bytes that are no external message can never be valid anywhere (rejected, proven).
      if (signed.raw.encoding !== 'base64') return MALFORMED;
      const expected = hashOfRaw(signed.raw.data);
      if (expected === null) return MALFORMED;
      try {
        const sent = await ctx.api.send(signed.raw.data, {
          ...BROADCAST,
          ...(options.fanout !== undefined ? { fanout: options.fanout } : {}),
          ...(options.signal ? { signal: options.signal } : {}),
        });
        if (sent.hashNorm !== expected) {
          ctx.log.warn('the node reports another normalized message hash', {
            code: 'MESSAGE_HASH_MISMATCH',
          });
        }
        return { kind: 'accepted' };
      } catch (error) {
        // Only a definitive answer is classified; anything ambiguous goes back unchanged
        // (R16, R17, lesson 3). toncenter's HTTP 500 refusals always land here.
        if (isCryptoAioError(error, 'RPC_ERROR') && !error.ambiguous) {
          return classifyBroadcastError(nodeText(error.details?.body) ?? error.message);
        }
        throw error;
      }
    },
  };
}
```

- [ ] **Step 4: Run the test to verify it passes**

Run: `pnpm jest test/adapters/ton/builder.test.ts`
Expected: PASS, 15 tests. "counts each forward fee once" pins I3 (`network = import + gas + storage + Σfwd`, once); "refuses a chain time far from the local clock" pins M3; "sends jettons to the wallet the proof quorum named" pins I5.

- [ ] **Step 5: Check and commit**

Run: `pnpm format && pnpm lint && pnpm typecheck && pnpm test`
Expected: all green.

```bash
git add src/adapters/ton/builder.ts test/adapters/ton/builder.test.ts
git commit -m "feat(ton): transfers: fees, funds, the signing request, assembly and broadcast

Co-Authored-By: Claude Opus 5.5 (1M context) <noreply@anthropic.com>"
```

**Review points:**
- The identity guard runs before any codec, signing or I/O (lesson 5); `CONFIG_INVALID` when the identity does not derive `from`.
- The built ordering carries exactly the allocated seqno and a `valid_until` from chain time, bounded against the local clock (M3); the SDK never reads the wall clock (D8).
- The `network` charge counts the forward fee once (I3), and `details.forwardFeeSource` says which source.
- The sender jetton wallet comes from a quorum read, never one endpoint's answer (I5).
- The `StateInit` is attached exactly when the wallet is undeployed; the bounce flag follows the recipient's variant, raw = bounceable (D7).
- `assemble` places the signature only where it fits the digest; the Attempt id is the TEP-467 hash, `canonical: false`.
- The broadcaster never classifies an ambiguous failure, and `rejected` only for bytes that are no external message or an anchored malformed text (D16).

### Task 10: Proofs and address history

**Files:**
- Create: `src/adapters/ton/proofs.ts`
- Test: `test/adapters/ton/proofs.test.ts`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `TonContext`, `decodeWithJettons`, `findOwnAttempt`, `confirmLegs` (Task 8); `MONITOR`, `PROOF`, `READ`, `runResultOf`, `BlockHeader`, `RunResult`, `V3Transaction`, `TonApi.lagTolerance` (Task 5); `attemptVerdict`, `isOwnAttempt` (Task 7); `decodeWalletRequest`, `messageBody` (Task 3); `TonNetworkConfig.finalitySkewBlocks` (Task 6).
- Produces:
  - `CONSUMER_PAGES = 8`.
  - `attestedHead(ctx): Promise<BlockHeader>`: lesson 17's one unanchored head: one endpoint's masterchain head minus `finalitySkewBlocks`, attested by a quorum read of that block (ids, hashes, time, global id); on a retryable failure retried once at the transport's lag tolerance (150), so a peer trailing further than the skew delays the proof instead of stalling it (M1).
  - `expiredAt(ctx, header, validUntil): Promise<boolean>` (D9: `valid_until` at or below the time of the masterchain block and of every shard top it commits).
  - `createTonProofs(ctx): ProofSource`:
    - `finalizedHead()`: the attested head.
    - `includedFinal(ref, ordering, from)`: our own wallet transaction under `ref` (`findOwnAttempt` under `PROOF`: the external message to `from` whose locally computed TEP-467 hash is `ref.id`, C1) → proven (complete trace, `reachedMasterchain(last trace block)` attested, the verdict confirmed by `confirmLegs` under `PROOF`, block hash at a fixed height, `reason` on failure). Otherwise the wallet's seqno at the attested head decides, read only from an `active` wallet, or an `uninitialized` one when our slot is seqno 0; any other state is undecided (I6): unconsumed and expired there → `included: false`; unconsumed and not expired → undecided; consumed → the consuming request (`provenRequest`: an external request, or a W5 `internal_signed` one the wallet ran, in both cases signed by the wallet's key at H for this very wallet; A23 and the final review), searched in a fixed lt window with bodies bound to their keyed hashes: ours (TEP-467 hash computed locally) → proven, another message → `included: false`, not found → undecided, logged as `SEQNO_CONSUMER_NOT_FOUND` once the window is exhausted. Undecided is always a retryable `PROVIDER_UNAVAILABLE` (lesson 16).
    - `slotConsumed(ordering, from, level)`: `latest` is always `false` (masterchain state is final, so TON never reports an observed `replaced`, I4); `finalized` attests "my seqno is above n" as a monotone predicate `quorumKey` (lesson 17).
    - `expired(ordering)`: `expiredAt` at the attested head.
    - `blockHash(height, level)`: `null` above the head; else the root hash of the masterchain block at that fixed height (quorum).
  - `createTonHistory(ctx): AddressHistorySource`: v3 account transactions newest-first, decoded with verified jettons; the cursor is the next `end_lt` (decimal); a malformed cursor is `INVALID_INTENT`.

- [ ] **Step 1: Write the failing test**

`test/adapters/ton/proofs.test.ts`:

```ts
import { Cell, loadMessage, type MessageRelaxed } from '@ton/core';
import { nativeMessage } from '../../../src/adapters/ton/messages';
import { createTonHistory, createTonProofs } from '../../../src/adapters/ton/proofs';

/** The built-in networks' `params.finalitySkewBlocks`. */
const FINALITY_SKEW = 10;
import { REASONS } from '../../../src/adapters/ton/trace';
import type { AttemptRef } from '../../../src/core/model/transaction';
import { tonHarness } from './support/context';
import { relayedBody, signedBoc, testWallet } from './support/harness';

const TESTNET = -3;
const GRAM = 1_000_000_000n;
const FRESH = `0:${'11'.repeat(32)}`;
const ref = (id: string): AttemptRef => ({
  id,
  idKind: 'message-hash',
  canonical: false,
});

function setup(options: Parameters<typeof tonHarness>[0] = {}) {
  const h = tonHarness(options);
  const proofs = createTonProofs(h.ctx);
  const from = testWallet('v4r2', TESTNET);
  h.node.fund(from, 5n * GRAM);
  const now = () => Math.floor(h.clock.now() / 1000);
  /** A signed request for seqno 0 from the test wallet; not sent. */
  const request = async (messages: MessageRelaxed[], validFor = 60) => {
    const validUntil = now() + validFor;
    const signed = await signedBoc('v4r2', TESTNET, {
      seqno: 0,
      validUntil,
      deploy: true,
      messages,
    });
    return { ...signed, ordering: { kind: 'seqno' as const, seqno: 0n, validUntil } };
  };
  const pay = (bounce = false) =>
    request([nativeMessage({ to: FRESH, value: GRAM, bounce })]);
  return { h, proofs, from, request, pay, now };
}

describe('TON proofs (lesson 17, final form)', () => {
  it('attests an unanchored head trailed by the skew', async () => {
    const s = setup({ endpoints: ['a', 'b'] });
    s.h.node.mine(FINALITY_SKEW + 5);
    const head = s.h.node.head;
    s.h.node.lagEndpoint('b', 3);
    const attested = await s.h.run(s.proofs.finalizedHead());
    expect(attested).toEqual({
      height: BigInt(head - FINALITY_SKEW),
      hash: s.h.node.block(head - FINALITY_SKEW)?.rootHash,
      timestamp: s.h.node.block(head - FINALITY_SKEW)?.genUtime,
    });
    s.h.node.intercept = (endpoint, route) =>
      endpoint === 'b' && route === '/getBlockHeader'
        ? {
            json: {
              ok: true,
              result: {
                id: {
                  workchain: -1,
                  shard: '-9223372036854775808',
                  seqno: head - FINALITY_SKEW,
                  root_hash: 'A'.repeat(43) + '=',
                  file_hash: 'A'.repeat(43) + '=',

          }

  it('attests a consumed seqno with a monotone predicate; a lagging peer decides nothing', async () => {
    const s = setup({ endpoints: ['a', 'b'] });

    expect(await s.h.run(s.proofs.slotConsumed(ordering, s.from, 'finalized'))).toBe(

    expect(await s.h.run(s.proofs.slotConsumed(ordering, s.from, 'finalized'))).toBe(

  it('decides nothing while the indexer lags behind the consumed seqno (Review Focus 2)', async () => {
    const s = setup({ node: { indexerLag: 40 } });

  it('proves expiry only once every shard is past valid_until (Review Focus 5)', async () => {
    const s = setup({ node: { shardLagSeconds: 30 } });

  it('retries the attested head at the lag tolerance when a peer trails further (M1)', async () => {
    const s = setup({
      endpoints: ['a', 'b'],
      maxLagBlocks: 150,
      node: { shardLagSeconds: 1 },
    });
    const { boc, hashNorm, ordering } = await s.pay();
    void boc; // never sent: the chain never sees it
    for (let i = 0; i < 260; i++) {

    s.h.node.lagEndpoint('b', 40);

    expect(

      included: false,

  });

  it('proves expiry only once the slowest of two shards is past valid_until (D9, M11)', async () => {
    const s = setup({
      node: { shards: 2, shardLagSeconds: 1, secondShardLagSeconds: 40 },
    });
    const { ordering } = await s.pay();

      for (let i = 0; i < seconds; i++) {
        await s.h.clock.advance(1_000);

    await tick(90);

      // The indexer loses our hash (so the consumer search runs) and swaps the body.
      const other = await s.request([
        nativeMessage({ to: FRESH, value: 7n, bounce: false }),
      ]);
      const swapped = Buffer.from(
        loadMessage(
          Cell.fromBoc(Buffer.from(other.boc, 'base64'))[0]!.beginParse(),
        ).body.toBoc(),
      ).toString('base64');
      s.h.node.intercept = (_endpoint, route, request) => {
        if (route === '/transactionsByMessage')
          return { json: { transactions: [], address_book: {} } };
        if (route !== '/transactions') return undefined;
        s.h.node.intercept = undefined;
        return s.h.node.fetch.fetch(request.url.href).then(async (response) => {
          const body = (await response.json()) as {
            transactions: {
              in_msg: { source: string | null; message_content: { body: string } };
            }[];
          };
          for (const tx of body.transactions) {
            if (tx.in_msg.source === null) tx.in_msg.message_content.body = swapped;
          }
          s.h.node.intercept = (_e, r) =>
            r === '/transactionsByMessage'
              ? { json: { transactions: [], address_book: {} } }
              : undefined;
          return { json: body };
        }) as never;
      };
      await expect(
        s.h.run(s.proofs.includedFinal(ref(ours.hashNorm), ours.ordering, s.from)),
      ).rejects.toMatchObject({ code: 'PROVIDER_UNAVAILABLE', retryable: true });
    });

    it('makes up an external consumer claiming our seqno: its signature fails, never "replaced"', async () => {
      const s = setup();
      const ours = await s.pay();
      s.h.node.submit(ours.boc);
      s.h.node.mine(FINALITY_SKEW + 3);
      // A lone lying indexer loses our hash and puts, on top of the wallet's history, an
      // external request for our seqno that another key signed.
      const madeUp = await signedBoc('v4r2', TESTNET, {
        seqno: 0,
        validUntil: s.now() + 60,
        deploy: false,
        messages: [nativeMessage({ to: FRESH, value: 7n, bounce: false })],
        seed: 'ab'.repeat(32),
      });
      const body = loadMessage(
        Cell.fromBoc(Buffer.from(madeUp.boc, 'base64'))[0]!.beginParse(),
      ).body;
      const b64 = (hex: string) => Buffer.from(hex, 'hex').toString('base64');
      const hideLookups = () => {
        s.h.node.intercept = (_e, r) =>
          r === '/transactionsByMessage'
            ? { json: { transactions: [], address_book: {} } }
            : undefined;
      };
      s.h.node.intercept = (_endpoint, route, request) => {
        if (route === '/transactionsByMessage')
          return { json: { transactions: [], address_book: {} } };
        if (route !== '/transactions') return undefined;
        s.h.node.intercept = undefined;
        return s.h.node.fetch.fetch(request.url.href).then(async (response) => {
          type Wire = {
            hash: string;
            in_msg: {
              source: string | null;
              hash: string;
              hash_norm?: string;
              message_content: { hash: string; body: string };
            };
          };
          const answer = (await response.json()) as { transactions: Wire[] };
          const real = answer.transactions.find((tx) => tx.in_msg.source === null);
          if (!real) throw new Error('no external request indexed');
          const fake = structuredClone(real);
          fake.hash = b64('ee'.repeat(32));
          fake.in_msg.hash = b64('ef'.repeat(32));
          fake.in_msg.hash_norm = b64(madeUp.hashNorm);
          fake.in_msg.message_content = {
            hash: body.hash().toString('base64'),
            body: body.toBoc().toString('base64'),
          };
          answer.transactions.unshift(fake);
          hideLookups();
          return { json: answer };
        }) as never;
      };
      expect(
        await s.h.run(s.proofs.includedFinal(ref(ours.hashNorm), ours.ordering, s.from)),
      ).toMatchObject({ included: true, success: true });
    });

    it("offers another wallet's transaction under our hash: not ours, no verdict", async () => {
      const s = setup();
      const ours = await s.pay();
      // Another wallet's payment lands; the indexer returns it for our hash.
      const otherWallet = testWallet('v5r1', TESTNET);
      s.h.node.fund(otherWallet, GRAM);
      const other = await signedBoc('v5r1', TESTNET, {
        seqno: 0,
        validUntil: s.now() + 60,
        deploy: true,
        messages: [nativeMessage({ to: FRESH, value: 5n, bounce: false })],
      });
      s.h.node.submit(other.boc);
      s.h.node.mine(3);
      s.h.node.intercept = (_endpoint, route, request) =>
        route === '/transactionsByMessage' &&
        request.url.searchParams.get('msg_hash') === ours.hashNorm
          ? (s.h.node.fetch
              .fetch(request.url.href.replace(ours.hashNorm, other.hashNorm))
              .then(async (response) => ({ json: await response.json() })) as never)
          : undefined;
      const seen = await s.h.run(
        s.h.reader.observe(ref(ours.hashNorm), ours.ordering, s.from),
      );
      expect(seen).toEqual({ seen: 'none' });
      await expect(
        s.h.run(s.proofs.includedFinal(ref(ours.hashNorm), ours.ordering, s.from)),
      ).rejects.toMatchObject({ code: 'PROVIDER_UNAVAILABLE' });
    });
  });

  describe('a relayed W5 request consumes the seqno only when proven (A23)', () => {
    const RELAYER = `0:${'22'.repeat(32)}`;
    /** A v5r1 test wallet, deployed by our own transfer for seqno 0. */
    async function deployed() {
      const h = tonHarness();
      const proofs = createTonProofs(h.ctx);
      const from = testWallet('v5r1', TESTNET);
      const validUntil = Math.floor(h.clock.now() / 1000) + 60;
      h.node.fund(from, 5n * GRAM);
      const ours = await signedBoc('v5r1', TESTNET, {
        seqno: 0,
        validUntil,
        deploy: true,
        messages: [nativeMessage({ to: FRESH, value: GRAM, bounce: false })],
      });
      h.node.submit(ours.boc);
      h.node.mine();
      const relay = (seqno: number, seed?: string) =>
        h.node.inject(
          RELAYER,
          from,
          50_000_000n,
          relayedBody(TESTNET, {
            seqno,
            validUntil,
            messages: [nativeMessage({ to: FRESH, value: 2n, bounce: false })],
            ...(seed !== undefined ? { seed } : {}),
          }),
        );
      return { h, proofs, from, validUntil, ours, relay };
    }
    /** The indexer's hash lookup misses (a race, or no normalized-hash lookup). */
    const hideLookups = (h: ReturnType<typeof tonHarness>) => {
      h.node.intercept = (_endpoint, route) =>
        route === '/transactionsByMessage'
          ? { json: { transactions: [], address_book: {} } }
          : undefined;
    };

    it('skips a forged request claiming our seqno after our transfer: never "replaced"', async () => {
      const { h, proofs, from, validUntil, ours, relay } = await deployed();
      relay(0, 'ab'.repeat(32)); // anyone can post it; the wallet ignores it
      h.node.mine(FINALITY_SKEW + 3);
      hideLookups(h);
      expect(
        await h.run(
          proofs.includedFinal(
            ref(ours.hashNorm),
            { kind: 'seqno', seqno: 0n, validUntil },
            from,
          ),
        ),
      ).toMatchObject({ included: true, success: true });
    });

    it("counts the owner's genuine relayed request, which the wallet ran", async () => {
      const { h, proofs, from, validUntil, relay } = await deployed();
      const next = await signedBoc('v5r1', TESTNET, {
        seqno: 1,
        validUntil,
        deploy: false,
        messages: [nativeMessage({ to: FRESH, value: 3n, bounce: false })],
      });
      relay(1); // the owner's gasless transfer takes seqno 1 first
      h.node.mine(FINALITY_SKEW + 3);
      expect(h.node.seqno(from)).toBe(2);
      hideLookups(h);
      expect(
        await h.run(
          proofs.includedFinal(
            ref(next.hashNorm),
            { kind: 'seqno', seqno: 1n, validUntil },
            from,
          ),
        ),
      ).toEqual({ included: false });
    });
  });

  it('reads block hashes at a height, and none above the head', async () => {
    const s = setup();
    s.h.node.mine(3);
    expect(await s.h.run(s.proofs.blockHash(2n, 'finalized'))).toBe(
      s.h.node.block(2)?.rootHash,
    );
    expect(
      await s.h.run(s.proofs.blockHash(BigInt(s.h.node.head + 1), 'latest')),
    ).toBeNull();
  });
});

describe('TON address history', () => {
  it('pages an address newest first with an lt cursor', async () => {
    const s = setup();
    const history = createTonHistory(s.h.ctx);
    for (const [seqno, value] of [
      [0, 1n],
      [1, 2n],
      [2, 3n],
    ] as const) {
      const { boc } = await signedBoc('v4r2', TESTNET, {
        seqno,
        validUntil: s.now() + 60,
        deploy: seqno === 0,
        messages: [nativeMessage({ to: FRESH, value, bounce: false })],
      });
      s.h.node.submit(boc);
      s.h.node.mine(2);
    }
    const first = await s.h.run(history.list(FRESH, { limit: 2 }));
    expect(first.items.map((tx) => tx.transfers[0]?.amount)).toEqual([3n, 2n]);
    expect(first.next).toMatch(/^\d+$/);
    const second = await s.h.run(history.list(FRESH, { limit: 2, cursor: first.next! }));
    expect(second.items.map((tx) => tx.transfers[0]?.amount)).toEqual([1n]);
    expect(second.next).toBeUndefined();
    await expect(
      s.h.run(history.list(FRESH, { limit: 2, cursor: 'x' })),
    ).rejects.toMatchObject({
      code: 'INVALID_INTENT',
    });
  });
});
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Write the proofs and history**

`src/adapters/ton/proofs.ts`:

```ts
/**
 * Proofs and address history (spec §6.7, §10, D9, D10, D12). Every proof follows lesson 17's
 * final form: a fact is attested at its own height, with a monotone predicate or at a fixed
 * block, and no endpoint proposes a height. The one exception is the unanchored head: one
 * endpoint's masterchain head, trailed by `finalitySkewBlocks` and attested by a quorum read
 * of that block, retried at the transport's own lag tolerance (M1). Anything not yet
 * decidable (indexer lag, an incomplete trace, a message that may still land, a wallet state
 * that does not show its seqno) throws a retryable `PROVIDER_UNAVAILABLE`, which decides
 * nothing (lesson 16).
 */
import type { AddressHistorySource, ProofSource } from '../../core/driver/types';
import {
  ProviderError,
  ValidationError,
  isCryptoAioError,
} from '../../core/errors/error';
import type { OrderingData } from '../../core/model/ordering';
import {
  MONITOR,
  PROOF,
  READ,
  runResultOf,
  type BlockHeader,
  type RunResult,
  type V3Transaction,
} from './api';
import {
  confirmLegs,
  decodeWithJettons,
  findOwnAttempt,
  provenRequest,
  publicKeyAt,
  type TonContext,
} from './reader';
import { attemptVerdict, isOwnAttempt } from './trace';

/** How far back the consumer of a seqno is searched: pages of 64 transactions. */
export const CONSUMER_PAGES = 8;

const undecided = (reason: string) =>
  new ProviderError('PROVIDER_UNAVAILABLE', reason, { retryable: true });

/**
 * The one unanchored head (lesson 17): one endpoint's head, trailed by the network's skew
 * and attested by the quorum; when a healthy peer trails further, retried at the transport's
 * lag tolerance, the furthest a proof endpoint may trail (M1). Never a false head, only a
 * later one.
 */
export async function attestedHead(ctx: TonContext): Promise<BlockHeader> {
  const head = await ctx.api.masterchainHead(MONITOR);
  const { finalitySkewBlocks: skew } = ctx.config;
  const lag = ctx.api.lagTolerance;
  const skews = lag > skew ? [skew, lag] : [skew];
  let last: unknown;
  for (const skew of skews) {
    try {
      return await ctx.api.masterchainHeader(Math.max(1, head - skew), PROOF);
    } catch (error) {
      if (!isCryptoAioError(error) || !error.retryable) throw error;
      last = error;
    }
  }
  throw last;
}

/**
 * D9: no block after `header` can include a message valid until `validUntil`: the
 * masterchain block and every shard top it commits are at least that old, and a wallet
 * rejects a message once `valid_until <= now()`.
 */
export async function expiredAt(
  ctx: TonContext,
  header: BlockHeader,
  validUntil: number,
): Promise<boolean> {
  if (validUntil > header.genUtime) return false;
  for (const shard of await ctx.api.shards(header.id.seqno, PROOF)) {
    if (validUntil > (await ctx.api.blockHeader(shard, PROOF)).genUtime) return false;
  }
  return true;
}

const seqnoIn = (result: RunResult): bigint | undefined => {
  const first = result.stack[0];
  return result.exitCode === 0 && first?.type === 'num' ? first.value : undefined;
};

/**
 * The wallet transaction that consumed `seqno`, searched newest-first below the wallet's
 * last transaction at the attested block `head` (a fixed window, so honest indexers agree).
 * Only a proven request counts (A23 and the final review): an external one, or a W5
 * request relayed internally that the wallet ran (M17), in either case signed by the
 * wallet's key at `head` for this very wallet. A forged or made-up request that claims the
 * seqno is skipped, so it can never pass for the consumer. `undefined` when the
 * consumer is not (yet) indexed.
 */
async function consumerOf(
  ctx: TonContext,
  from: string,
  seqno: bigint,
  head: BlockHeader,
  lastLt: bigint,
): Promise<V3Transaction | undefined> {
  const keyAtHead = publicKeyAt(ctx, from, PROOF, head.id.seqno);
  const key = () => keyAtHead();
  let endLt = lastLt;
  for (let page = 0; page < CONSUMER_PAGES; page++) {
    const txs = await ctx.api.accountTransactions(from, { limit: 64, endLt }, PROOF);
    for (const tx of txs) {
      const request = await provenRequest(ctx, tx, from, key);
      if (!request) continue;
      if (BigInt(request.seqno) === seqno) return tx;
      if (BigInt(request.seqno) < seqno) return undefined;
    }
    const last = txs.at(-1);
    if (!last || txs.length < 64) return undefined;
    endLt = last.lt - 1n;
  }
  ctx.log.warn('the seqno consumer is beyond the search window', {
    code: 'SEQNO_CONSUMER_NOT_FOUND',
  });
  return undefined;
}

type Included = Extract<
  Awaited<ReturnType<ProofSource['includedFinal']>>,
  { included: true }
>;

/** Our indexed transaction, proven: complete trace, reached by the liteservers, verdict. */
async function proveIncluded(
  ctx: TonContext,
  tx: V3Transaction,
  from: string,
): Promise<Included> {
  const trace = await ctx.api.trace(tx.hash, PROOF);
  if (!trace || !trace.complete) throw undecided('the message trace is not complete yet');
  const last = Math.max(tx.mcSeqno, ...trace.transactions.map((t) => t.mcSeqno));
  // Lesson 17: the fact "block `last` exists" attested with a monotone predicate.
  if (!(await ctx.api.reachedMasterchain(last, PROOF))) {
    throw undecided('the endpoints have not reached the trace yet');
  }
  const verdict = await confirmLegs(ctx, attemptVerdict(tx, trace), from, PROOF);
  if (verdict.kind === 'pending')
    throw undecided('the message trace is not complete yet');
  const header = await ctx.api.masterchainHeader(tx.mcSeqno, PROOF);
  return {
    included: true,
    success: verdict.kind === 'success',
    blockHeight: BigInt(tx.mcSeqno),
    blockHash: header.id.rootHash,
    txHash: tx.hash,
    ...(verdict.kind === 'failed' ? { reason: verdict.reason } : {}),
  };
}

const seqnoOrdering = (ordering: OrderingData) =>
  ordering.kind === 'seqno' ? ordering : undefined;

export function createTonProofs(ctx: TonContext): ProofSource {
  const { api } = ctx;
  return {
    async finalizedHead() {
      const header = await attestedHead(ctx);
      return {
        height: BigInt(header.id.seqno),
        hash: header.id.rootHash,
        timestamp: header.genUtime,
      };
    },

    async includedFinal(ref, ordering, from) {
      // C1: only our own wallet transaction, its hash computed here from the bound body.
      const own = await findOwnAttempt(ctx, ref, from, PROOF);
      if (own) return proveIncluded(ctx, own, from);
      const slot = seqnoOrdering(ordering);
      if (!slot) throw undecided('a TON attempt needs its seqno ordering');
      // Not indexed under our hash. Decide only from state at an attested block (D12).
      const head = await attestedHead(ctx);
      const state = await api.account(from, PROOF, head.id.seqno);
      let seqno: bigint | undefined;
      if (state.status === 'active') {
        seqno = seqnoIn(await api.runGetMethod(from, 'seqno', [], PROOF, head.id.seqno));
      } else if (state.status === 'uninitialized' && slot.seqno === 0n) {
        seqno = 0n; // never deployed: our first message did not run
      }
      // I6: a frozen wallet, or an uninitialized one past seqno 0 (deleted), shows no seqno.
      if (seqno === undefined)
        throw undecided('the wallet state does not show its seqno');
      if (seqno <= slot.seqno) {
        // Unconsumed at `head`: absent for good only once nothing later can include it.
        if (await expiredAt(ctx, head, slot.validUntil)) return { included: false };
        throw undecided('the message may still be included');
      }
      const consumer = await consumerOf(ctx, from, slot.seqno, head, state.lastLt);
      if (!consumer) {
        throw undecided('the transaction that consumed the seqno is not indexed yet');
      }
      if (isOwnAttempt(consumer, from, ref.id)) return proveIncluded(ctx, consumer, from);
      return { included: false };
    },

    async slotConsumed(ordering, from, level) {
      const slot = seqnoOrdering(ordering);
      if (!slot) return false;
      // I4: masterchain state is final once it exists, so TON gives no separate `latest`
      // evidence: our own landed-but-unindexed message is never observed as `replaced`.
      if (level === 'latest') return false;
      const above = (result: RunResult): boolean => {
        const value = seqnoIn(result);
        return value !== undefined && value > slot.seqno;
      };
      // Lesson 17: "my seqno at the final state is above n", a monotone predicate.
      return above(
        await api.runGetMethod(from, 'seqno', [], {
          ...PROOF,
          quorumKey: (body) => above(runResultOf(body)),
        }),
      );
    },

    async expired(ordering) {
      const slot = seqnoOrdering(ordering);
      if (!slot) return false;
      return expiredAt(ctx, await attestedHead(ctx), slot.validUntil);
    },

    async blockHash(height) {
      // Every masterchain block is final once it exists: both levels read the same.
      if (height > BigInt(await api.masterchainHead(MONITOR))) return null;
      return (await api.masterchainHeader(Number(height), PROOF)).id.rootHash;
    },
  };
}

export function createTonHistory(ctx: TonContext): AddressHistorySource {
  return {
    async list(address, options) {
      if (options.cursor !== undefined && !/^\d{1,20}$/.test(options.cursor)) {
        throw new ValidationError('INVALID_INTENT', 'not a TON history cursor');
      }
      const account = ctx.codec.normalize(address).canonical;
      const txs = await ctx.api.accountTransactions(
        account,
        {
          limit: options.limit,
          ...(options.cursor !== undefined ? { endLt: BigInt(options.cursor) } : {}),
        },
        READ,
      );
      const items = [];
      for (const tx of txs) items.push(await decodeWithJettons(ctx, tx, READ));
      const last = txs.at(-1);
      return {
        items,
        ...(last && txs.length === options.limit
          ? { next: (last.lt - 1n).toString() }
          : {}),
      };
    },
  };
}
```

- [ ] **Step 4: Run the test to verify it passes**

Run: `pnpm jest test/adapters/ton/proofs.test.ts`
Expected: PASS, 17 tests. "decides nothing while the indexer lags behind the consumed seqno" pins Review Focus 2; "proves expiry only once every shard is past valid_until" pins Review Focus 5's expiry half; the three `a lying first endpoint` tests pin C1 (a flipped bounce, a swapped body, another wallet's transaction: never a verdict); "retries the attested head at the lag tolerance" pins M1; the two-shard expiry test pins D9 with M11's second shard; "never reads a frozen wallet as seqno 0" pins I6; the `latest` assertion in the predicate test pins I4; the two relayed-request tests pin A23 (a forged request claiming our seqno after our transfer never makes it `replaced`; the owner's genuine relayed request still counts); "makes up an external consumer claiming our seqno" pins the external signature check (a lone lying indexer).

- [ ] **Step 5: Check and commit**

Run: `pnpm format && pnpm lint && pnpm typecheck && pnpm test`
Expected: all green.

```bash
git add src/adapters/ton/proofs.ts test/adapters/ton/proofs.test.ts
git commit -m "feat(ton): proofs over attested masterchain state, and address history

Co-Authored-By: Claude Opus 5.5 (1M context) <noreply@anthropic.com>"
```

**Review points:**
- Lesson 17 (final form): every fact is attested at its own height (a fixed block, or a monotone predicate such as "seqno above n" or "block R reached"); only `attestedHead` starts from one endpoint's head, trailed by the skew and attested; no quorum compares data read at a moving head.
- `included: false` only when the seqno is unconsumed at an attested block where the message is already expired, or consumed by another message found in a fixed window; indexer lag, an incomplete trace, an unreachable block, or a wallet state that does not show its seqno always decide nothing (lesson 16, I6). With bound data this closes core residual R76 for TON.
- Every input is bound (C1): our Attempt by a locally computed hash, every body by its keyed hash, a trace by a key over all of it; a liar can only make proofs decide nothing.
- `slotConsumed(…, 'latest')` is `false`, so no observed `replaced` ever appears for our own landed message (I4).
- Expiry uses shard time (D9), not masterchain time or the local clock.
- The consumer's identity is our own TEP-467 computation over a bound body, not the indexer's `hash_norm` (D12, C1); every consumer must be signed by the wallet's key for this very wallet, and a W5 relayed one must also have run (A23, the final review), so a forged relayed body or a made-up external one is skipped.
- History uses the indexer with `read` tags and decodes jettons only for verified wallets.

### Task 11: The driver factory and the native client

**Files:**
- Create: `src/adapters/ton/driver.ts`, `src/adapters/ton/native-client.ts`
- Test: `test/adapters/ton/driver.test.ts`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: every port of Tasks 5–10; `tonNetworkConfig` (Task 6); `MAX_MESSAGES`, `resolveIdentity` (Task 3); `DriverFactory`, `DriverContext`, `DisposableNativeClient`, `HealthProbes`, `EndpointCall`, `Transport`, `PLACEHOLDER_ORIGIN`; `@ton/ton` `TonClient` (in `native-client.ts` only). Task 4's node answers `TonClient`'s `/jsonRPC` path.
- Produces (Plan 2's binding family shape, I2):
  - `tonDriverFactory(makeNative: (transport: Transport) => DisposableNativeClient): DriverFactory` in `driver.ts`, which imports no client SDK. `create(ctx)`: validates the network, refuses a missing indexer (`CONFIG_INVALID`), calls `setProbes` exactly once on `transport` (v2: identity = config param 19 as a decimal global id; height = the masterchain head) and on `indexer` (v3: identity = the indexed head's `global_id`; height = the newest indexed masterchain block) before any traffic (M12, R19), and assembles `ordering: 'seqno'`, `capabilities`, `address` (the context's codec), `reader`, `builder`, `broadcaster`, `proofs`, `sequence`, `history`, `ext: { ton: { getSeqno, jettonWallet } }`, `limits(wallet)` (4 / 255; 1 only without a `ton` identity, a malformed one throws, M18) and `createNativeClient: () => makeNative(ctx.transport)`. No `blocks` (D21), no `replacement`; it never reads `network.maxLagBlocks` (R36: the pool resolves it).
  - `native-client.ts`, the library module: `tonNativeClient(transport): DisposableNativeClient`, a fresh `TonClient({ endpoint: PLACEHOLDER_ORIGIN + '/jsonRPC', httpAdapter })` on every call, whose adapter posts each request to the transport (`/jsonRPC`, `read` tags), nothing to close; and `tonLibraryDriverFactory = tonDriverFactory(tonNativeClient)`, which the manifest's `load()` returns (Task 12).

```ts
import { TonClient } from '@ton/ton';
import { TON_CHAINS } from '../../../src/adapters/ton/chains';
import { tonDriverFactory } from '../../../src/adapters/ton/driver';
import {
  tonLibraryDriverFactory,
  tonNativeClient,
} from '../../../src/adapters/ton/native-client';
import type { DriverContext } from '../../../src/core/driver/types';
import { noopLogger } from '../../../src/core/events/logger';
import type { ChainInfo, NetworkInfo } from '../../../src/core/model/chain';
import type { Transport } from '../../../src/core/transport/types';
import { tonNode } from './support/harness';
import { PUBLIC_KEY, TEST_WALLETS } from './support/vectors';

const chain = TON_CHAINS[0] as ChainInfo;
const PK = Buffer.from(PUBLIC_KEY, 'hex');

/** Counts `setProbes` calls and HTTP traffic on a transport (M12). */
function counting(transport: Transport) {
  const log: string[] = [];
  const proxy: Transport = new Proxy(transport, {
    get(target, prop, receiver) {
      const value = Reflect.get(target, prop, receiver) as unknown;
      if (typeof value !== 'function') return value;
      if (prop === 'setProbes' || prop === 'http') {
        return (...args: unknown[]) => {
          log.push(String(prop));
          return (value as (...a: unknown[]) => unknown).apply(target, args);
        };
      }
      return value.bind(target);
    },
  });
  return { proxy, log };
}

async function driverFor(networkId: 'mainnet' | 'testnet', nodeGlobalId?: number) {
  const network = chain.networks[networkId] as NetworkInfo;
  const t = tonNode({ globalId: nodeGlobalId ?? Number(network.identity) });
  const rpc = counting(t.rpc);
  const indexer = counting(t.indexer);
  const ctx: DriverContext = {
    chain,
    network,
    library: '@ton/ton',
    transport: rpc.proxy,
    indexer: indexer.proxy,
    clock: t.clock,
    log: noopLogger,
    options: {},
  };
  const driver = await tonLibraryDriverFactory.create(ctx);
  return { t, driver, rpc, indexer, ctx };
}

describe('the TON driver factory', () => {
  it('sets probes exactly once on both transports before any traffic (M12)', async () => {
    const { rpc, indexer } = await driverFor('testnet');
    expect(rpc.log).toEqual(['setProbes']);
    expect(indexer.log).toEqual(['setProbes']);
  });

  it('refuses to run without an indexer', async () => {
    const network = chain.networks.testnet as NetworkInfo;
    const t = tonNode();
    await expect(
      tonLibraryDriverFactory.create({
        chain,
        network,
        library: '@ton/ton',
        transport: t.rpc,
        clock: t.clock,
        log: noopLogger,
        options: {},
      }),
    ).rejects.toMatchObject({
      code: 'CONFIG_INVALID',
      message: expect.stringContaining('indexer'),
    });
  });

  it('assembles the seqno driver: no block source, no replacement, history, ext and limits', async () => {
    const { driver } = await driverFor('testnet');
    expect(driver.ordering).toBe('seqno');
    expect([...driver.capabilities].sort()).toEqual([
      'address-history',
      'batch-transfer',
      'expiry',
      'memo',
      'tokens',
    ]);
    expect(driver.blocks).toBeUndefined();
    expect(driver.replacement).toBeUndefined();
    expect(driver.history).toBeDefined();
    expect(Object.keys(driver.ext?.ton ?? {}).sort()).toEqual([
      'getSeqno',
      'jettonWallet',
    ]);
    expect(driver.limits?.({ ton: { version: 'v4r2' } })).toEqual({ maxOutputs: 4 });
    expect(driver.limits?.({ ton: { version: 'v5r1' } })).toEqual({ maxOutputs: 255 });
    expect(driver.limits?.({})).toEqual({ maxOutputs: 1 });
    // M18: a malformed identity is its CONFIG_INVALID, never a silent 1.
    expect(() => driver.limits?.({ ton: { version: 'v9' } })).toThrow(
      expect.objectContaining({ code: 'CONFIG_INVALID' }),
    );
  });

  it("derives v5r1 wallets from the network's global id (Review Focus 4)", async () => {
    const testnet = (await driverFor('testnet')).driver;
    const mainnet = (await driverFor('mainnet')).driver;
    const v5 = { ton: { version: 'v5r1' } };
    expect(testnet.address.fromPublicKey(PK, v5).canonical).toBe(
      TEST_WALLETS.v5r1.testnet,
    );
    expect(mainnet.address.fromPublicKey(PK, v5).canonical).toBe(
      TEST_WALLETS.v5r1.mainnet,
    );
    expect(() =>
      testnet.address.fromPublicKey(PK, {
        ton: { version: 'v5r1', networkGlobalId: -239 },
      }),
    ).toThrow(expect.objectContaining({ code: 'CONFIG_INVALID' }));
  });

  it('checks both endpoints: the global id as identity, the masterchain head as height (R19)', async () => {
    const { t, rpc, indexer } = await driverFor('testnet');
    t.node.mine(3);
    await t.run(rpc.proxy.refreshHealth());
    await t.run(indexer.proxy.refreshHealth());
    expect(rpc.proxy.status()).toEqual([
      expect.objectContaining({ state: 'healthy', height: 4n }),
    ]);
    expect(indexer.proxy.status()).toEqual([
      expect.objectContaining({ state: 'healthy', height: 4n }),
    ]);
    const wrong = await driverFor('testnet', -239);
    await wrong.t.run(wrong.rpc.proxy.refreshHealth());
    await wrong.t.run(wrong.indexer.proxy.refreshHealth());
    expect(wrong.rpc.proxy.status()[0]?.state).toBe('disabled');
    expect(wrong.indexer.proxy.status()[0]?.state).toBe('disabled');
  });

  it('gives each native client its own TonClient over the transport (R34)', async () => {
    const { t, driver } = await driverFor('testnet');
    const first = driver.createNativeClient!();
    const second = driver.createNativeClient!();
    expect(first.client).toBeInstanceOf(TonClient);
    expect(first.client).not.toBe(second.client);
    t.node.mine(2);
    const info = await t.run((first.client as TonClient).getMasterchainInfo());
    expect(info.latestSeqno).toBe(t.node.head);
    expect(t.node.served.at(-1)).toEqual({ endpoint: 'main', route: '/jsonRPC' });
  });

  it('takes the family shape: tonDriverFactory(makeNative), the library module supplies it (I2)', async () => {
    const made: Transport[] = [];
    const factory = tonDriverFactory((transport) => {
      made.push(transport);
      return tonNativeClient(transport);
    });
    const network = chain.networks.testnet as NetworkInfo;
    const t = tonNode();
    const driver = await factory.create({
      chain,
      network,
      library: '@ton/ton',
      transport: t.rpc,
      indexer: t.indexer,
      clock: t.clock,
      log: noopLogger,
      options: {},
    });
    driver.createNativeClient!();
    expect(made).toEqual([t.rpc]);
  });

  it('reads seqnos through ext.ton', async () => {
    const { t, driver } = await driverFor('testnet');
    const read = driver.ext?.ton?.getSeqno as (address: string) => Promise<bigint>;
    expect(await t.run(read(TEST_WALLETS.v4r2.basechain))).toBe(0n);
  });
});
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Write the driver factory**

`src/adapters/ton/driver.ts`:

```ts
/**
 * The TON driver (spec §15): one factory for both built-in networks, configured by each
 * network's registry data. The family shape (Plan 2 Task 10, lesson 17 board entry):
 * `tonDriverFactory(makeNative)` lives here and imports no client module; the library's
 * module (`native-client.ts`) supplies the native client and exports the loadable factory.
 */
import { Cell } from '@ton/core';
import type {
  ChainDriver,
  DisposableNativeClient,
  DriverFactory,
  WalletOptions,
} from '../../core/driver/types';
import { ConfigError, ProviderError } from '../../core/errors/error';
import type { EndpointCall, HealthProbes, Transport } from '../../core/transport/types';
import { TonApi } from './api';
import { createTonBroadcaster, createTonBuilder } from './builder';
import { tonNetworkConfig } from './network';
import { createTonHistory, createTonProofs } from './proofs';
import {
  createTonContext,
  createTonExt,
  createTonReader,
  createTonSequence,
} from './reader';
import { MAX_MESSAGES, resolveIdentity } from './wallets';

const malformedProbe = (what: string) =>
  new ProviderError('PROVIDER_UNAVAILABLE', `malformed ${what} answer`);

const record = (value: unknown): Record<string, unknown> =>
  value !== null && typeof value === 'object' ? (value as Record<string, unknown>) : {};

/** v2: identity = config param 19 (the global id); height = the masterchain head (R19). */
const RPC_PROBES = (expectedIdentity: string | undefined): HealthProbes => ({
  identity: async (call: EndpointCall) => {
    const body = record(
      await call.http({
        method: 'GET',
        path: '/getConfigParam',
        query: { param: '19' },
        route: '/getConfigParam',
      }),
    );
    const bytes = record(record(body.result).config).bytes;
    if (body.ok !== true || typeof bytes !== 'string')
      throw malformedProbe('config param');
    try {
      const cell = Cell.fromBoc(Buffer.from(bytes, 'base64'))[0] as Cell;
      return String(cell.beginParse().loadInt(32));
    } catch {
      throw malformedProbe('config param');
    }
  },
  ...(expectedIdentity !== undefined ? { expectedIdentity } : {}),
  height: async (call: EndpointCall) => {
    const body = record(
      await call.http({
        method: 'GET',
        path: '/getMasterchainInfo',
        route: '/getMasterchainInfo',
      }),
    );
    const seqno = record(record(body.result).last).seqno;
    if (body.ok !== true || !Number.isSafeInteger(seqno))
      throw malformedProbe('masterchain');
    return BigInt(seqno as number);
  },
});

/** v3: identity = the indexed head's global id; height = the newest indexed block. */
const INDEXER_PROBES = (expectedIdentity: string | undefined): HealthProbes => {
  const last = async (call: EndpointCall) =>
    record(
      record(
        await call.http({
          method: 'GET',
          path: '/masterchainInfo',
          route: '/masterchainInfo',
        }),
      ).last,
    );
  return {
    identity: async (call) => {
      const id = (await last(call)).global_id;
      if (!Number.isSafeInteger(id)) throw malformedProbe('indexer');
      return String(id);
    },
    ...(expectedIdentity !== undefined ? { expectedIdentity } : {}),
    height: async (call) => {
      const seqno = (await last(call)).seqno;
      if (!Number.isSafeInteger(seqno)) throw malformedProbe('indexer');
      return BigInt(seqno as number);
    },
  };
};

/**
 * The batch limit of a wallet version (spec §5.5); 1 without a TON identity. A malformed
 * identity throws its `CONFIG_INVALID` (M18).
 */
function limitsOf(
  wallet: WalletOptions,
  globalId: number,
): { readonly maxOutputs: number } {
  if (wallet.ton === undefined) return { maxOutputs: 1 };
  return { maxOutputs: MAX_MESSAGES[resolveIdentity(wallet, globalId).version] };
}

/** The TON driver factory over a native-client maker (the library module supplies it). */
export const tonDriverFactory = (
  makeNative: (transport: Transport) => DisposableNativeClient,
): DriverFactory => ({
  async create(ctx): Promise<ChainDriver> {
    const config = tonNetworkConfig(ctx.chain, ctx.network);
    if (!ctx.indexer) {
      throw new ConfigError(
        'CONFIG_INVALID',
        'TON needs an indexer provider (toncenter API v3); set chains.ton.indexer',
      );
    }
    // M12: probes go on every transport this driver receives, before any traffic.
    ctx.transport.setProbes(RPC_PROBES(ctx.network.identity));
    ctx.indexer.setProbes(INDEXER_PROBES(ctx.network.identity));
    const ton = createTonContext({
      api: new TonApi(ctx.transport, ctx.indexer),
      chain: ctx.chain,
      network: ctx.network,
      config,
      log: ctx.log,
      clock: ctx.clock,
    });
    const ext = createTonExt(ton);
    return {
      ordering: 'seqno',
      capabilities: config.capabilities,
      address: ton.codec,
      reader: createTonReader(ton),
      builder: createTonBuilder(ton),
      broadcaster: createTonBroadcaster(ton),
      proofs: createTonProofs(ton),
      sequence: createTonSequence(ton),
      history: createTonHistory(ton),
      ext: { ton: { getSeqno: ext.ton.getSeqno, jettonWallet: ext.ton.jettonWallet } },
      limits: (wallet) => limitsOf(wallet, config.globalId),
      createNativeClient: () => makeNative(ctx.transport),
    };
  },
});
```

Then the library module, which alone names the client SDK.

`src/adapters/ton/native-client.ts`:

```ts
/**
 * The `@ton/ton` library module (the family shape: the client module exports the loadable
 * factory). The native client is a `TonClient` whose `httpAdapter` posts to the transport
 * (spec §11), off the driver's own request path (D1). SDKs are required by their bare peer
 * names (R80).
 */
import { TonClient } from '@ton/ton';
import type { DisposableNativeClient } from '../../core/driver/types';
import { PLACEHOLDER_ORIGIN, type Transport } from '../../core/transport/types';
import { READ } from './api';
import { tonDriverFactory } from './driver';

type HttpAdapter = NonNullable<ConstructorParameters<typeof TonClient>[0]['httpAdapter']>;

/** A fresh `TonClient` over `transport` on every call (R34); `crypto-aio/native` only. */
export function tonNativeClient(transport: Transport): DisposableNativeClient {
  const httpAdapter = (async (config: { data?: unknown }) => {
    const body =
      typeof config.data === 'string'
        ? (JSON.parse(config.data) as unknown)
        : config.data;
    const data = await transport.http(
      { method: 'POST', path: '/jsonRPC', body, route: '/jsonRPC' },
      READ,
    );
    return { data, status: 200, statusText: 'OK', headers: {}, config, request: {} };
  }) as unknown as HttpAdapter;
  return {
    client: new TonClient({ endpoint: `${PLACEHOLDER_ORIGIN}/jsonRPC`, httpAdapter }),
  };
}

/** The `@ton/ton` adapter's driver factory; the manifest's `load()` returns it. */
export const tonLibraryDriverFactory = tonDriverFactory(tonNativeClient);
```

**Review points:**
- `setProbes` exactly once on both transports, before any traffic (counting Proxy); identity is the network's global id on both APIs; a wrong-network endpoint is disabled.
- The capability set never contains `block-scan`, `replace-fee` or `cancel`; `blocks` and `replacement` are absent.
- `createNativeClient` returns a new `TonClient` each call, over the transport (no URL or key reaches the SDK); the driver itself never calls `TonClient` (D1), and `driver.ts` never imports it: only `native-client.ts` does (I2).
- `limits` returns 1 only when `wallet.ton` is absent; a malformed identity throws `CONFIG_INVALID` (M18).
- The driver holds only immutable-data caches; no wallet or tenant state.

```ts
describe('the built-in TON plugin', () => {

    // R82: the ranges are the package's own peer ranges.

      'presets',
      'tokens',
      'address',
      'api',

      'fees',
      'errors',
    ]) {
      expect([
        file,
        /from '@ton\//.test(readFileSync(join(dir, `${file}.ts`), 'utf8')),
      ]).toEqual([file, false]);
    }
  });
});
```
